title: Pack startup defaults and applied policy preparation
tool: Compiled recipe source, planner and activation fixtures, entry-point checks and GitHub Actions receipts
---

Each registered pack must now supply its own compiled product startup recipe. The recipe owns the automatic total ceiling, context, draft mode/placement/depth, lookahead policy, prefix enablement, short-prompt behavior and GPU keep-alive choice. The original recipe moves the existing Desktop defaults without enlarging them. The hardware ceiling stays independent of current headroom; a custom limit may exceed that recommendation inside the supported range and keeps its exact value. Complete startup admission uses the selected pack's resource ledger and exact integer byte comparisons. Required components and missing memory observations remain refusal conditions.

Desktop planning and range display now consume the frozen pack's recipe. Controls price required components as part of the hardware range even before download; load separately verifies actual presence. Telemetry keys the range by the startup policy as well as the manifest and hardware, and derives the displayed selected ceiling from the same retained pack. This does not yet replace the installed-content-aware Auto proposal/selection work, and the current app loader still admits only the original registered pack.

An explicit Engine startup method applies the compiled product settings under the generation gate before publication. Independent Engine and CLI users retain their prior defaults unless they call this method. Desktop's named product recipe now sets draft depth, speculation enablement and GPU keep-alive explicitly instead of inheriting ambient tuning of those controls. Applied product configuration identity includes the complete startup policy, alongside actual model arithmetic, correction content, plan and executable identity. A later mutation cannot still be identified as the unchanged recipe. Older receipts decode with absent startup-policy observations; independent callers retain the prior identity form.

Durable activation selections freeze the startup-policy identity. Unknown or superseded recipes cannot be loaded or used for rollback. Older original-pack records map only to their historical original policy. Equality preserves that compatibility so adding the explicit field cannot accidentally turn an earlier failed attempt into a different request eligible for automatic retry. Requested choices, budget and live management remain independent and unchanged.

New pure native fixtures compare the original complete plan with the legacy planner, distinguish a recipe's own cap from the original cap, preserve a larger custom limit and fractional controls, check busy/unknown hardware, validate recipe bounds, bind changed execution settings and decode legacy applied receipts. App fixtures check owned planning, durable recipe mismatch and legacy equivalence. The existing real activation check now requires applied-policy identity and actual bounded prefill observations, but it has not run on this source. The external-consumer probe exercises the additive API while preserving old function-value signatures. Thirty-two entry-point checks and shell/diff syntax checks pass locally. No native compilation or model process was started for this stage.

The prior V2 performance source at 689eea7f15591f9904a70e6b2498a8be97a3e4db passes its complete instrumented catalogue, including the Auto matcher, corrected range checks and expanded performance protocol. The captured whole-engine and Mac snapshots remain in progress, with external-library and Xcode already successful. Those results do not certify the current startup-recipe edits. The held-out quality continuation remains frozen, running and unanalyzed. Complete quality and performance, standalone production and public pull, qualified Auto proposals, installed acceptance, alternative app loading and final integrated acceptance remain open.
